Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on mathematical_statistics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 791. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 928 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_791(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 791. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^928(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any mathematical_statistics module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 576. The projective resolution has length at most 745 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the mathematical_statistics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^576 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 745-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
